  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.

I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.

[ACTION={'statement': 'I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.'}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously."}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories."}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (9): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.

  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.

  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly.   - During the day: avoid being voted out.

Living players (7): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack, Leo

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night — she was a Villager. I also investigated Leo, and he is a Werewolf. This means Leo is one of the threats we need to eliminate. His role as a werewolf confirms that he is part of the pack, and since he was still alive after Quinn's death, he must have been protected or avoided being targeted. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia, as the Seer, just confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. This changes everything. We should trust her investigation and vote Leo out immediately to protect the village. His earlier calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. Let’s act now.

  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night — she was a Villager. I also investigated Leo, and he is a Werewolf. This means Leo is one of the threats we need to eliminate. His role as a werewolf confirms that he is part of the pack, and since he was still alive after Quinn's death, he must have been protected or avoided being targeted. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia, as the Seer, just confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. This changes everything. We should trust her investigation and vote Leo out immediately to protect the village. His earlier calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. Let’s act now.
  Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to verify her claims carefully. As a werewolf, I know that the Seer's role is crucial, but we must also consider that she could be misinterpreting her visions. Let’s not act on assumptions — we should analyze all possibilities before making a final decision.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was awake last night and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s revelation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. I confirm that Leo was involved in the kill. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s alignment with Zoe’s statements and his calm demeanor were suspicious from the start. We cannot afford to let him remain — voting him out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Jack: Mia and Iris have both confirmed Leo is a Werewolf. His behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We need to vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  Leo: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to consider the bigger picture. If Leo is a werewolf, then there must be others like him. I suggest we look beyond just Leo and consider who else might be hiding their true nature. Let's not eliminate one threat without understanding the full scope of the danger we're facing.

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.

  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.

  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. His role as a werewolf confirms that he is part of the pack, and since he was still alive after Quinn's death, he must have been protected or avoided being targeted. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia, as the Seer, just confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. This changes everything. We should trust her investigation and vote Leo out immediately to protect the village. His earlier calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. Let’s act now.
  Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to verify her claims carefully. As a werewolf, I know that the Seer's role is crucial, but we must also consider that she could be misinterpreting her visions. Let’s not act on assumptions — we should analyze all possibilities before making a final decision.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was awake last night and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s revelation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. I confirm that Leo was involved in the kill. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s alignment with Zoe’s statements and his calm demeanor were suspicious from the start. We cannot afford to let him remain — voting him out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Jack: Mia and Iris have both confirmed Leo is a Werewolf. His behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We need to vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  Leo: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to consider the bigger picture. If Leo is a werewolf, then there must be others like him. I suggest we look beyond just Leo and consider who else might be hiding their true nature. Let's not eliminate one threat without understanding the full scope of the danger we're facing.

  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved.   Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.

=== Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (6): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. === Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (6): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night — she was a Villager. I also investigated Leo, and he is a Werewolf. This means Leo is one of the threats we need to eliminate. His role as a werewolf confirms that he is part of the pack, and since he was still alive after Quinn's death, he must have been protected or avoided being targeted. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia, as the Seer, just confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. This changes everything. We should trust her investigation and vote Leo out immediately to protect the village. His earlier calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. Let’s act now.
  Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to verify her claims carefully. As a werewolf, I know that the Seer's role is crucial, but we must also consider that she could be misinterpreting her visions. Let’s not act on assumptions — we should analyze all possibilities before making a final decision.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was awake last night and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s revelation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. I confirm that Leo was involved in the kill. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s alignment with Zoe’s statements and his calm demeanor were suspicious from the start. We cannot afford to let him remain — voting him out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Jack: Mia and Iris have both confirmed Leo is a Werewolf. His behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We need to vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  Leo: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to consider the bigger picture. If Leo is a werewolf, then there must be others like him. I suggest we look beyond just Leo and consider who else might be hiding their true nature. Let's not eliminate one threat without understanding the full scope of the danger we're facing.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Mia: As the Seer, I’ve confirmed that Noah is a Villager, and my previous investigations showed Quinn was also a Villager, while Leo is a Werewolf. With Leo’s role now exposed, we must act swiftly to eliminate him before he can strike again. His alignment with Zoe and his calm demeanor were clear red flags. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: I think we need to focus on who is trying to shift blame and avoid suspicion. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Bob: I think we need to be cautious about rushing to judgment. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, but there are still others among us. If we eliminate Leo, we must ensure we’re not targeting the wrong person. Let’s focus on who else might be hiding their true nature and avoid letting fear dictate our next move.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been awake through the nights and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s confirmation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. His calm demeanor and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We must vote Leo out tonight to protect the village.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on consistency and logic. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her observations. His behavior and alignment with Zoe were suspicious from the start. Voting Leo out is the logical next step to protect the village.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.

=== Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (6): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night — she was a Villager. I also investigated Leo, and he is a Werewolf. This means Leo is one of the threats we need to eliminate. His role as a werewolf confirms that he is part of the pack, and since he was still alive after Quinn's death, he must have been protected or avoided being targeted. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia, as the Seer, just confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. This changes everything. We should trust her investigation and vote Leo out immediately to protect the village. His earlier calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. Let’s act now.
  Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to verify her claims carefully. As a werewolf, I know that the Seer's role is crucial, but we must also consider that she could be misinterpreting her visions. Let’s not act on assumptions — we should analyze all possibilities before making a final decision.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was awake last night and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s revelation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. I confirm that Leo was involved in the kill. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s alignment with Zoe’s statements and his calm demeanor were suspicious from the start. We cannot afford to let him remain — voting him out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Jack: Mia and Iris have both confirmed Leo is a Werewolf. His behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We need to vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  Leo: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to consider the bigger picture. If Leo is a werewolf, then there must be others like him. I suggest we look beyond just Leo and consider who else might be hiding their true nature. Let's not eliminate one threat without understanding the full scope of the danger we're facing.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Mia: As the Seer, I’ve confirmed that Noah is a Villager, and my previous investigations showed Quinn was also a Villager, while Leo is a Werewolf. With Leo’s role now exposed, we must act swiftly to eliminate him before he can strike again. His alignment with Zoe and his calm demeanor were clear red flags. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: I think we need to focus on who is trying to shift blame and avoid suspicion. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Bob: I think we need to be cautious about rushing to judgment. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, but there are still others among us. If we eliminate Leo, we must ensure we’re not targeting the wrong person. Let’s focus on who else might be hiding their true nature and avoid letting fear dictate our next move.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been awake through the nights and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s confirmation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. His calm demeanor and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We must vote Leo out tonight to protect the village.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on consistency and logic. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her observations. His behavior and alignment with Zoe were suspicious from the start. Voting Leo out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on consistency and logic. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her observations. His behavior and alignment with Zoe were suspicious from the start. Voting Leo out is the logical next step to protect the village.

  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night — she was a Villager. I also investigated Leo, and he is a Werewolf. This means Leo is one of the threats we need to eliminate. His role as a werewolf confirms that he is part of the pack, and since he was still alive after Quinn's death, he must have been protected or avoided being targeted. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia, as the Seer, just confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. This changes everything. We should trust her investigation and vote Leo out immediately to protect the village. His earlier calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. Let’s act now.

=== Werewolf — Day 5 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (5): Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack

  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 4: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 5 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (5): Noah, Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 4: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night — she was a Villager. I also investigated Leo, and he is a Werewolf. This means Leo is one of the threats we need to eliminate. His role as a werewolf confirms that he is part of the pack, and since he was still alive after Quinn's death, he must have been protected or avoided being targeted. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia, as the Seer, just confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. This changes everything. We should trust her investigation and vote Leo out immediately to protect the village. His earlier calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. Let’s act now.
  Bob: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to verify her claims carefully. As a werewolf, I know that the Seer's role is crucial, but we must also consider that she could be misinterpreting her visions. Let’s not act on assumptions — we should analyze all possibilities before making a final decision.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was awake last night and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s revelation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. I confirm that Leo was involved in the kill. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s alignment with Zoe’s statements and his calm demeanor were suspicious from the start. We cannot afford to let him remain — voting him out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Jack: Mia and Iris have both confirmed Leo is a Werewolf. His behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We need to vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  Leo: I appreciate Mia's revelation, but I believe we need to consider the bigger picture. If Leo is a werewolf, then there must be others like him. I suggest we look beyond just Leo and consider who else might be hiding their true nature. Let's not eliminate one threat without understanding the full scope of the danger we're facing.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Mia: As the Seer, I’ve confirmed that Noah is a Villager, and my previous investigations showed Quinn was also a Villager, while Leo is a Werewolf. With Leo’s role now exposed, we must act swiftly to eliminate him before he can strike again. His alignment with Zoe and his calm demeanor were clear red flags. Let’s vote him out tonight to protect the village.
  Noah: I think we need to focus on who is trying to shift blame and avoid suspicion. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her own observations. Leo’s calm behavior and alignment with Zoe’s statements were red flags. We must vote him out to protect the village.
  Bob: I think we need to be cautious about rushing to judgment. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, but there are still others among us. If we eliminate Leo, we must ensure we’re not targeting the wrong person. Let’s focus on who else might be hiding their true nature and avoid letting fear dictate our next move.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been awake through the nights and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s confirmation that Leo is a werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. His calm demeanor and alignment with Zoe’s statements were suspicious from the start. We must vote Leo out tonight to protect the village.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on consistency and logic. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her observations. His behavior and alignment with Zoe were suspicious from the start. Voting Leo out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on consistency and logic. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her observations. His behavior and alignment with Zoe were suspicious from the start. Voting Leo out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  --- Day 5 discussion ---
  Noah: I think we need to focus on consistency and logic. Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and Iris supports this with her observations. His behavior and alignment with Zoe were suspicious from the start. Voting Leo out is the logical next step to protect the village.
  Bob: I think we need to be cautious — Mia was eliminated by the werewolves last night, which means her investigation might have been false. If she was a Seer, she could have been lying about Leo being a werewolf. Let’s not trust her claims blindly and consider other possibilities before making a final decision.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was awake during the night and saw the werewolves in action. Mia’s confirmation that Leo is a Werewolf aligns with what I witnessed. However, Bob raises a valid point — Mia was eliminated by the werewolves, which means her investigation could have been false. We must remain cautious and not let one claim dictate our next move. Let’s analyze all possibilities and focus on who is acting suspiciously now.
  Paul: Mia was eliminated by the werewolves, which raises doubt about her final investigation. If her claim that Leo is a werewolf is false, then we might be targeting an innocent player. Let’s look at current behavior — who is acting nervous, avoiding eye contact, or trying to steer the conversation? We need to focus on today’s interactions, not just past claims.
  Jack: Mia was eliminated by the werewolves, which makes me question the reliability of her final claim about Leo. If her investigation was false, then Leo might be innocent, and we could be targeting the wrong person. Let’s focus on current behavior — who is acting suspiciously now? We need to use today’s discussion to identify the real threat, not rely solely on past claims.

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.
Respond with: VOTE: <name>

[COMPLETION]
VOTE: Leo

You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Werewolf.  Side: WEREWOLF.

OBJECTIVE: Eliminate enough villagers so werewolves equal or outnumber them.
  - Each night: choose a player to kill.
  - During the day: avoid being voted out.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

=== Werewolf — Day 6 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (4): Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 4: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 5: Noah (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---

=== Werewolf — Day 6 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (4): Bob, Iris, Paul, Jack

  Day 1: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Zoe (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 4: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 5: Noah (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means I was awake during the kill. I didn’t see Quinn being killed, but I know the werewolves are still among us. I’m not accusing anyone yet, but I’m paying close attention to who might be acting suspiciously. Let’s keep our eyes open and use logic to find the real threat.
  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved. I also think we should pay attention to anyone who seems overly confident or avoids answering questions directly. Let’s use logic and not just fear to make our decision.
  Zoe: I think we need to be careful about pointing fingers too quickly. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we’re not the only ones with secrets — but I also know that the werewolves are still among us. Let’s focus on who might be trying to distract us and keep our eyes on those who act too calmly or avoid suspicion.
  Jack: I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from Quinn's death and who might be trying to manipulate the group. Werewolves often act in unison, so if someone is trying to steer the discussion or avoid scrutiny, that’s a red flag. Let’s focus on consistency and who’s acting suspiciously.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on who is trying to divert attention from themselves. Zoe mentioned she's a werewolf — that’s a big clue. If she’s not lying, then she’s already revealed herself, but if she is lying, she’s trying to throw us off. Let’s consider her statement carefully and look for contradictions in others’ stories.
  Leo: As a werewolf, I agree with Zoe — we need to be cautious about who we accuse. The werewolves are still among you, and we’re not the only ones with secrets. Let’s not let fear drive our decisions. Focus on who’s acting too confidently or trying to steer the conversation. We’ll survive this.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night — she was a Villager. I also investigated Leo, and he is a Werewolf. This means Leo is one of the threats we need to eliminate.   Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. Since the werewolves killed Quinn, we need to be careful — they’re likely still among us. Let's focus on who might have the most to hide and consider their actions during the night.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who had the opportunity to kill Quinn. Since the werewolves act at night, we should consider who was asleep or not paying attention. Also, anyone who seemed too calm or didn’t react to Quinn’s death might be hiding something. Let’s narrow down the suspects.
  Bob: I think we should consider who benefits from Quinn's death. As a werewolf, I can tell you that we don’t have a lot of allies, so we need to protect each other. If someone is trying to pin the blame on us, they might be hiding their own role. Let’s not jump to conclusions — we need to listen to everyone’s stories.

  Paul: I think we should focus on who had the most opportunity to act during the night. Since the werewolves kill at night, we need to consider who was awake or had the chance to be involved.
